4:T45d,Agora is built around three constraints absent in centralized training: (1) the model is sharded primarily via pipeline parallelism, using subspace networks to compress cross-stage activations by roughly two orders of magnitude so that PP is viable over internet-grade links; (2) data-parallel replicas do not synchronize gradients with a per-step all-reduce; instead, sparse asynchronous parameter averaging exchanges only a small subset of slow-moving weights, infrequently and in the background, so weight synchronization overlaps with computation and never blocks the optimizer step; (3) fault tolerance is built into the training step rather than implemented as an external recovery mechanism, so joins, leaves, and heterogeneity are handled continuously, and newcomers catch up from stale weights without disrupting the run. On top of this, the system adopts ideas from Unextractable Protocol Models (UPM): because the model is model-parallel, no single participant ever holds the full weights, aiming to make weight extraction from individual nodes infeasible. The overall paradigm is termed Protocol Learning.5:T5d4,The contribution is not a new optimizer but a tightly controlled reassessment. The setup has three important pieces. First, the authors choose problem families that can be systematically swept: symmetric PSD factorization, matrix completion, and NMF. These cover unconstrained low-rank recovery, missing-observation settings, and constrained factorization with nonnegativity and redundancy. Second, they run equal-budget, carefully tuned comparisons across Muon, Adam, AdamW, and GD, instead of inheriting community-default hyperparameters. This matters because Muon is sensitive to learning rate, momentum, weight decay, and the approximation details of the orthogonalization step. Third, they interpret results through the lens of what orthogonalization is actually doing. Muon approximately orthogonalizes matrix-shaped gradients, flattening singular-value anisotropy and moving closer to steepest descent under the spectral norm. One might expect that to help on spectral problems, but the paper shows that in classical matrix factorization this does not automatically beat coordinate-wise adaptivity; AdamW’s per-coordinate scaling is often more useful under ill-conditioning. Where Muon does help is NMF: orthogonalized updates appear to discourage factor collapse and reduce redundant directions, leading to more diverse factors. So the gain may come less from faster fitting of dominant singular directions and more from an implicit regularization effect on representation geometry.6:T418,The experimental contribution is about relative behavior across regimes rather than a single headline number. Four findings stand out. First, on standard low-rank matrix factorization, Muon’s advantage mostly vanishes after fair tuning, with AdamW and GD reaching similar or better convergence and final error. Second, on matrix completion, Muon again does not show a stable lead, which weakens the simple intuition that spectral normalization should automatically help on spectrally structured tasks. Third, on NMF, Muon shows a more consistent advantage, especially in overparameterized settings or when factor redundancy is more severe, yielding better reconstruction and less factor overlap. Fourth, ill-conditioning alone does not hand the win to Muon; in many cases AdamW’s adaptive scaling remains stronger. The abstract truncates before detailed numbers, but the full paper’s value is the systematic sweep over conditioning, parameterization, and task variants, with conclusions grounded in tuned comparisons rather than one-off runs.7:T4c8,The method decomposes total GRPO post-training compute into C_search, C_learning, and C_reward. C_search covers autoregressive rollout generation. C_learning covers policy optimization, including an effective update-cost model for LoRA that accounts for trainable adapters as well as the frozen backbone forward and activation costs. C_reward covers reward or verifier inference, which is negligible for rule-based rewards but can be substantial for learned feedback such as process reward models. The main allocation variable is the update fraction ρ = C_learning / C_total. Low ρ corresponds to search- or feedback-heavy runs; high ρ corresponds to update-heavy runs. This differs from common reporting practice because reward-model inference is not treated as free. A PRM-based run and a rule-based run can have the same total FLOPs while buying different amounts of exploration and learning. The empirical setting uses LoRA-adapted Qwen2.5 policies trained with GRPO on mathematical reasoning tasks, and compares model sizes, allocation ratios, reward systems, and evaluation targets. RACE is introduced as a pilot-grid protocol for locating useful allocation regimes before spending budget on larger validation runs.8:T440,The experiments use GRPO post-training of LoRA-adapted Qwen2.5 policies on math reasoning tasks. Four findings are central. First, model size and allocation are coupled under a fixed budget: larger policies cost more per token, so the same budget yields fewer rollouts and fewer updates. As a result, a larger policy is not automatically the best post-training choice. Second, the best observed update fraction changes with model size; no single search-versus-learning split dominates. Third, reward design changes the compute frontier. Rule-based rewards leave most non-update compute for policy rollout, while learned feedback spends part of the budget on reward-model inference. Fourth, evaluation target matters. Native training reward, pass@1 symbolic accuracy, and common-judge process scores can select different allocations. The available text does not expose reproducible numerical tables, exact model-size lists, or absolute gain values, so the safest reading is that the empirical evidence supports conditional allocation frontiers rather than a single winning operating point.9:T60f,The framework treats a discrete diffusion model as a corruption-denoising system over a constructed state space. That state space may come from BPE or subword tokenizers, bytes or characters, VQ image tokens, codec audio tokens, video tokens, or natural scientific alphabets such as amino acids, nucleotides, atom types, and bond types. Within this lens, several families become different choices in the same design space. Transition-matrix methods, as in D3PM-style models, specify categorical transitions directly and can support uniform, nearest-neighbor, or structure-aware corruption. Masking or absorbing-state methods introduce a mask token as an absorbing state and train the model to recover clean tokens from partially corrupted sequences; they are related to masked language modeling, but generation proceeds through iterative denoising rather than one-shot filling. Score- or ratio-based methods learn discrete probability ratios or denoising directions rather than continuous scores, covering recent diffusion and flow-matching language models such as SEDD, MDLM, and LLaDA-like systems. The framework also explains why embed-then-diffuse is fragile: adding Gaussian noise in embedding space and rounding back to tokens creates a mismatch between continuous geometry and discrete vocabulary structure. Compared with AR modeling, native discrete diffusion gives up the simplicity of left-to-right likelihood and mature decoding infrastructure, but gains parallel updates, global revision, and a natural interface for infilling and editing.a:T44b,The work is primarily a unifying framework rather than a new large-scale empirical training study. Its evidence is comparative and organizational, spanning diffusion language models for text and code, tokenized multimodal generation, and scientific sequences or structured alphabets. Several practical lessons are emphasized. Token granularity changes the denoising problem: subword tokenization shortens sequences but makes each token semantically larger, while byte or character tokenization increases length but regularizes the alphabet. Vocabulary topology matters: in image or audio codebooks, uniform corruption can destroy local structure that a topology-aware transition matrix could preserve. Absorbing-mask models are convenient for infilling and arbitrary-order generation, but they require careful sampling schedules, confidence-based remasking, or step allocation to reduce train-sample mismatch. Parallel generation is not automatically faster in wall-clock terms; the number of denoising steps, denoiser size, resampling policy, and hardware efficiency determine the realized latency.b:T643,The technical core is to make the extra amplification from repeated visits to shared layers explicit in a first-order perturbation analysis. The starting point is DeepNorm: in Post-LN Transformers, one chooses a skip scale alpha and a residual-branch initialization gain beta so that the cumulative effect of parameter updates on the forward signal stays bounded. For untied depth, the condition is roughly M(beta/alpha)^2 = O(1), where M = 2N counts residual sublayer applications. In a looped Transformer, however, the same physical sublayer is visited R times, so both gradient writing and parameter reading are shared across rounds. The authors introduce a visit-alignment coefficient kappa_R to measure how strongly gradients and sensitivities align across visits. If visits decorrelate, kappa_R stays small and the analysis reduces to the DeepNorm case up to constants. If visits are coherent, kappa_R grows with R, and the shared update is effectively amplified. This yields a loop-aware stability condition kappa_R M(beta/alpha)^2 = O(1). Under the scaling family alpha = (cN)^p and beta = (dN)^(-p), the usual DeepNorm threshold p = 1/4 is recovered in the decorrelated regime, but in the conservative aligned regime with fixed physical depth K and growing loop count R, the threshold shifts to p = 1/2. Based on this, the paper proposes DeepLoop: keep the DeepNorm Post-LN architecture, but use the more conservative scaling alpha = (2N)^(1/2) and beta = (8N)^(-1/2). No gates, auxiliary losses, or learned residual coefficients are added; it is a one-line scaling rule tailored to tied depth.c:T4c5,The main diagnosis uses Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, with replication across four model families. The datasets include synthetic_json, compact JSON, synthetic XML, and wikitable inputs. Evaluation is exact match over leaf-value or table QA, with cache budgets from 5% to 100%. On synthetic JSON, average per-token attention mass is 13.64 for DELIM, 0.434 for KEY, 0.336 for VALUE, 0.177 for PROSE, and 0.623 for whitespace, so delimiter mass is roughly 40 times value mass. Under H2O, KEY tokens are retained about 1.94x, 1.79x, and 1.65x more often than VALUE tokens at 5%, 10%, and 20% budgets. On wikitable, HEADER retention reaches 47% at a 5% budget. Accuracy drops from 88% full-cache EM to 0% at a 5% H2O budget. Counterfactual eviction is the strongest evidence: banning KEY tokens improves performance, reaching 0.82 EM at B=0.5 and 0.88 at B=0.75 on 8k synthetic JSON, while banning DELIM or VALUE collapses to 0% under all sub-full budgets. Windowed SnapKV alone closes 81% of the H2O gap at B=0.5, and adding max pooling closes the gap at 16k context. The role-conditional allocator matches or slightly exceeds full-cache accuracy at higher budgets, although the authors note that this small gain is seed-sensitive.d:T4c3,The optimization targets the MiMo-V2.5 family, which combines Hybrid SWA, sparse MoE, and multimodal encoders. For MiMo-V2.5-Pro, the document describes a 70-layer model with 10 full-attention layers and 60 SWA layers, using a window size of 128. The central system change is a KV-cache refactor. Instead of a single KV pool that forces all layers to reserve O(N) sequence storage, the system separates full-attention KV from SWA KV. Full-attention layers retain full-sequence keys and values, while SWA layers keep only window-local entries, enforcing O(W) storage for the SWA part. A unified logical sequence view is still exposed to prefix trees, schedulers, and transport layers, with full-attention indices used as the authoritative reference and mappings maintained from full positions to SWA positions. Admission control checks both full and SWA cache constraints. The broader pipeline adds layerwise prefetching, SWA-aware prefix-cache trees, specialized placement policies, an RDMA-optimized distributed cache named GCache, and a KV-cache-affinity router to trade off cache reuse against load balance. Multimodal encoder throughput is treated as another bottleneck, especially for large images and long videos.e:T5ca,The central tool is the Visual Dependency Gap (VDG). For each question, the model is evaluated under the original-video condition and under a black-screen condition; VDG is defined from the difference in correctness between the two. This per-question formulation is important because it allows the benchmark to be stratified by actual dependence on visual input rather than only by aggregate averages. The paper then uses paired McNemar tests to show statistical dissociation: if two models differ significantly on original videos but not on black screens, higher accuracy cannot be equated with stronger visual grounding. Beyond the black-screen control, the authors build a four-step diagnostic ladder: black screen, single frame, shuffled frames, and original video. This decomposes what the benchmark is rewarding: any visual content at all, static spatial information, frame diversity, and temporal order. An FPS ablation from 0.5 to 24 FPS provides an additional probe into temporal sensitivity. The paper also revisits compression robustness through CRF sweeps and argues that flat robustness curves can be an artifact of benchmark composition, where visually dependent and visually independent questions cancel each other. The study spans 20 models, 10 architecture families, and 2B–78B parameters, including open-weight and API systems. The authors are careful not to overclaim from 4-bit quantized runs and restrict defended scaling conclusions to full-precision models.f:T640,The most important findings are diagnostic rather than leaderboard-oriented. On MVBench, InternVL2-8B significantly outperforms Qwen2-VL-7B under the original-video condition, with McNemar p = 0.0003, yet the same pair is statistically indistinguishable under black screens, with p = 0.53. That is the clearest evidence that accuracy and visual dependency are separable. Across 20 models, the ranking of task types by VDG is highly stable: Attribute Perception is the most visually dependent category at roughly 0.41, while Temporal Reasoning is close to the language-only baseline at roughly 0.06. The taxonomy also transfers across benchmarks: Video-MME labels reportedly predict MVBench classification perfectly for the mapped categories (7/7), and the pattern generalizes to EgoSchema. The four-condition ladder shows that temporal ordering contributes near-zero accuracy across open-weight models, whereas frame diversity matters much more; in practice, shuffling frames often hurts little, while collapsing to a single frame hurts more. The paper also highlights a diversity-extraction collapse in Qwen3-VL relative to Qwen2.5-VL, showing that newer models are not necessarily better at using video-specific information. In compression experiments, flat CRF curves are shown to be misleading because visually dependent questions are disproportionately compression-sensitive, and their degradation can be masked by many low-dependency questions. The benchmark suite includes a 600-question stratified subset of Video-MME, 462 MVBench questions, and EgoSchema, with models ranging from 2B to 78B.10:T42f,This one is worth a careful read for its evaluation methodology. Start with the introduction and the formal definition of VDG so the target quantity is clear: the paper is measuring dependence on visual input, not some vague notion of multimodal quality. Then go straight to the McNemar dissociation section, which is the strongest statistical argument in the paper. After that, read the task-type VDG spectrum and the cross-benchmark consistency analysis to understand which categories are structurally vulnerable to language-only solving. Next, study the four-condition ladder and the FPS ablation, since that is where the paper decomposes video information into spatial content, frame diversity, and temporal order. The compression section is also worth reading because it explains why apparent robustness can be a benchmark artifact. If you work on video pretraining or benchmark design, pay more attention to the deltas between black screen, single frame, shuffled frames, and original video than to raw accuracy rankings. A focused read takes about 45–60 minutes.11:T606,There are three central design choices. First, the task is a 26-week factored POMDP with six hidden factor processes governing demand, supply, logistics, quality, and related risks. Each week the agent observes a dashboard of symptoms—inventory, in-transit orders, realized demand, freight quotes, berth waiting times, canal transit counts, supplier scorecards, and quality reports—then chooses replenishment actions. Second, the hidden dynamics are intentionally factorized and the event tape is fixed, which makes a fair reference policy tractable: an exact Bayes filter is run per factor, and a rollout policy acts on the same observation stream seen by the LLM. The key point is not that the oracle is strong, but that it is non-privileged. Third, the benchmark defines decomposed metrics instead of relying on total cost alone. A skill score places the agent between a symptom-blind base-stock floor and the fair Bayesian oracle. Detection lag measures how quickly the agent names the active hidden factor in its rationale. Knowing-doing rate measures how often the agent still incurs stockout-like action failures during stress weeks it has already diagnosed correctly. The benchmark also introduces isolated, persistent, and compound stress profiles, keeping the world model fixed while varying overlap among hidden failures. One useful detail is that models are tested with a rules-only prompt and no strategy playbook, which makes the benchmark more about converting diagnosis into action than following a hand-crafted procedure.12:T5ba,The evaluation covers 50 seeds and 200 episodes, grouped by stress profile, with Claude Sonnet 5, GPT-5.4, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, and Grok 4.5. The main empirical result is a strong mismatch between diagnosis and control. On the perception side, all four models detect hidden-factor episodes at broadly similar rates, around 84% to 88%, typically within one week of onset. In some cases, the lower-skill models even diagnose faster. On the cost side, however, performance diverges sharply: two of the four models finish below the symptom-blind floor overall, meaning that on roughly half of their seeds, a policy that ignores symptoms entirely and follows a fixed inventory rule would have been cheaper. This is the clearest evidence that action quality, not just inference quality, is the main failure source. On persistent seeds, even during stress weeks that were correctly diagnosed, every model still suffers stockout in about 34% to 43% of those weeks. The paper further notes a severity effect: the weeks severe enough to be easy to detect are also the weeks most likely to break the shelf. Even after accounting for that, the main separation between models remains the quality of mapping diagnosis into order quantity, route, supplier choice, and protective actions. The knowing-doing gap is also bidirectional: some models under-react and order too little too late, while others over-protect and pay too much through freight locks, air expediting, or inspections.13:T502,The core idea is a three-layer abstraction. Benchmark handles dataset loading, task definition, and scoring. Harness wraps a model into an interactive agent, including prompt formatting, state management, multi-turn tool use, and provider-specific API handling. Environment provides the isolated execution context, including command execution, file transfer, text editing, and network services. These components are connected through stable interfaces and configured through a declarative RunRequest. The request is further decomposed into BenchmarkSpec, HarnessSpec, EnvironmentSpec, ModelSpec, and ExecutionSpec, separating evaluation semantics from execution mechanics. The framework uses lightweight decorator-based registries so new benchmarks, harnesses, or environments can be added locally without modifying the central runtime. A practically useful feature is scorer execution mode: scoring can happen in memory, reuse the agent workspace, or run in a fresh isolated environment, which is especially relevant for coding agents and patch verification. On the systems side, AgentCompass includes a fault-tolerant asynchronous runtime for parallel long-horizon trajectories and trajectory-level analyzers that expose intermediate actions, tool calls, and environment feedback.14:T87d,The paper is structured as a failure analysis plus a validation protocol. While building a bilingual Turkish/English faithfulness-judgment corpus, the authors accidentally shared one decoding-budget parameter across both judging calls and generation calls. That budget was fine for judges that only needed a short output, but disastrous for generators that were supposed to produce paragraph-length hallucinated answers. As a result, one producer’s Turkish negative examples were silently truncated into very short fragments. Those corrupted items then entered the judge evaluation pipeline and produced what looked like a strong cross-lingual bias effect.

The main contribution is not merely finding a bug. It is explaining why standard checks failed to catch it. The authors applied four aggregate-level robustness procedures, and all of them reinforced the false story rather than exposing the fault, because they only inspected judge behavior and not the stimuli themselves. The paper then contrasts two corpus-construction regimes:
1. LLM-generated-negative design: negatives are sampled from a model, so there is no cheap oracle to tell whether a short or odd output is a valid hallucination or a degenerate artifact.
2. Mechanical-perturbation design: negatives are derived from gold answers by deterministic transformations, so the relation between gold and negative can be checked mechanically, for example with string-level comparisons.

To turn this from an argument into evidence, the paper includes a positive control. The authors inject the closest mechanical analogue of their fault into a minimal oracle-bearing corpus and show that a simple gold-to-negative string comparison catches it with 100% accuracy, at zero API cost and zero human reading. For settings where oracle-less generation cannot be avoided, they propose a practical validation protocol: before computing any aggregate statistic, manually inspect 15 to 20 raw generated items per generation condition and report their length distribution and degeneration rate. The protocol is deliberately simple because, in their case, simple manual inspection was the only thing that actually worked.15:T626,The empirical core is a real A/B-verified pipeline failure. On the corrupted corpus, one judge appeared to suffer a 32-point collapse in selection accuracy in Turkish relative to English. The effect replicated from N=50 to N=500. The authors could even build a three-layer mechanistic account for it, and a controlled producer-swap experiment also supported the apparent story. Yet the effect was fabricated by stimulus corruption.

The corrected-data analysis is especially useful. The paper shows that generation faults can corrupt conclusions in two qualitatively different ways: they can fabricate an effect that does not exist, or they can distort a real effect into the wrong magnitude or shape. Aggregate metrics cannot distinguish these cases. So even when a bias is not entirely fictional, upstream stimulus degeneration can still bend the measurement in a misleading direction.

The positive-control experiment then demonstrates the design asymmetry. When an analogous fault is injected into a mechanical-perturbation corpus, a simple string-based oracle detects it with 100% accuracy and no additional API calls or human effort. This does not prove that mechanical designs are always better overall, but it does show a clear auditability advantage.

The limitations are also clear. The paper does not audit the broader literature to estimate how many existing judge corpora are affected, and it does not prove that inspecting 15 to 20 items is universally sufficient. It is best read as a well-validated failure analysis rather than a large-scale benchmark study.16:T588,For each unlabeled prompt, the current policy samples N candidate solutions. Final answers are extracted, the majority answer is identified, and one rollout that reaches that answer is selected as the consensus solution. A frozen snapshot of the model serves as teacher and is conditioned on the prompt plus this consensus solution, giving it privileged information unavailable to the student. The student only sees the original prompt and is trained on its own sampled tokens. The loss is a full-vocabulary Jensen–Shannon divergence between teacher and student next-token distributions on those student tokens. Several design choices matter. The teacher is stronger only through conditioning, not through parameter updates, which keeps optimization stable compared with on-policy RL. Distillation happens on the student’s own trajectory rather than forcing exact imitation of the teacher chain, reducing exposure mismatch. JSD is used instead of a one-sided KL to make matching less brittle when the teacher distribution is sharp or when consensus is imperfect. The whole pipeline reuses a single generation pass to obtain both consensus and the distillation substrate, and training runs for one epoch. The oracle variant, where the teacher is conditioned on gold-verified solutions, is especially informative because it quantifies how much of true privileged context can be recovered from self-consensus alone.17:T6d7,The evaluation has two regimes. In the transductive setting, training is done directly on the unlabeled prompts of the test benchmark and evaluation is on the same prompts, matching the common test-time RL setup. In the inductive setting, training uses one unlabeled prompt set and evaluation is on disjoint, often harder benchmarks. The headline result is that Canon is the strongest label-free method among those compared on the accuracy-versus-compute frontier. The paper reports up to about +12 pass@1 points on competition math and graduate-level science benchmarks in the transductive setting, with roughly one hour of GPU training, whereas RL-based label-free alternatives require around 6–10 hours. Relative to the oracle gold-conditioned teacher, Canon trails by only about 0.5 points on AMC, 3 points on AIME 2024, and 8 points on GPQA, suggesting that self-consensus captures a substantial fraction of the value of gold privileged context. The gains are not merely probability reallocation onto already-known answers: after training, the model solves some problems it had never solved in 32 attempts before training, and majority voting itself becomes more accurate on unsaturated benchmarks. The per-prompt analysis is one of the most useful parts: gains are largest when the base model’s majority answer is correct but uncertain, near zero when the task is already saturated, and negative when the majority is confidently wrong. The excerpt does not expose every model size, rollout count, or ablation table, but the full paper appears to include comparisons against self-consistency fine-tuning, test-time RL, gold-reward RL, rejection fine-tuning, and the oracle distillation variant across both transductive and inductive settings.18:T49c,LOTAPO constructs self-generated process rewards with backward leave-one-turn attribution. For a completed search trajectory, each valid search turn consists of the model-generated search action and the corresponding retrieval observation. The method replaces one such turn with a fixed [DELETE] placeholder while keeping the downstream interactions intact, then measures how the current policy’s mean log-likelihood of the gold answer changes. This Answer-Likelihood Gain estimates the turn’s contribution in the full reasoning context: if removing a turn lowers the gold-answer likelihood, the turn receives positive attribution; if removal increases it, the turn may be redundant or misleading. The raw gains are noisy, so LOTAPO applies robust scaling, a bounded tanh transform, group normalization, and sign-consistency gating. The gate keeps only process advantages whose normalized direction agrees with the raw likelihood-gain direction. The resulting process advantage is assigned to the policy-generated tokens of the corresponding search turn and combined with the terminal GRPO advantage, while the final answer turn remains supervised only by the terminal reward.19:T442,The method builds on contrastive persona-vector extraction from prior activation-steering work. For each trait, natural-language positive and negative descriptions are used to derive a behavioral direction in activation space; interventions along that direction are then tested during generation. The main addition is the diagnostic taxonomy. A trait is labeled natural if it appears at baseline, steerable if it can be amplified by intervention, and intractable if the standard protocol cannot recover a usable direction. The authors construct a 53-trait inventory across clinician interaction, generic behavioral disposition, elementary education, and agentic task completion. They also study pairwise composition by applying two persona vectors jointly over all 171 unordered pairs from 19 generic traits, looking for clean composition, dominance, or collapse. A safety-related case study shows that an “evil” direction that cannot be extracted from a safety-tuned model can still be transferred from a fine-tuned variant, suggesting that intractability is partly protocol-dependent.1a:T528,The analysis is built around the effective rank of the Jacobian at initialization, examining how each feedforward block component modulates rank preservation: 1. Skip connections are reinterpreted as rank-preserving mechanisms: the full-rank identity path bypasses rank loss in the residual branch. The relative scale of the branch vs. skip path controls a tradeoff between rank collapse and ensemble-like behavior — when all gradient flows through the skip path, the network degenerates into an ensemble of independent layers and loses deep compositional capacity. 2. Normalization placement regulates this same branch-to-skip ratio: Post-Norm keeps the ratio constant, leading to monotonic rank collapse with depth; Pre-Norm lets the ratio decay with depth, so rank plateaus at a moderate level, but deeper layers shift toward the ensemble regime. 3. The two-matrix (up-projection + down-projection) feedforward structure applies nonlinear activation in the expanded hidden dimension, where enough directions survive to maintain full residual branch Jacobian rank despite activation-induced rank loss; the down-projection also decorrelates branch means to avoid representation collapse onto a single direction. 4. Effective rank of the input-output Jacobian at initialization is used to predict network trainability.1b:T4e8,Targeted Parameter Decomposition (tPD) introduces a high-rank catch-all component Δ that handles all non-target data, so only mechanisms active on the target subset need to be explicitly decomposed into sparse rank-1 subcomponents. Key design details: 1. Training uses two parallel data streams: a small target dataset (inputs to be explained) and a non-target stream sampled from the model's original training distribution. 2. For target data, outputs are reconstructed using only a sparse set of rank-1 subcomponents, which capture mechanisms active on the target and do not need to sum to the full original weights. 3. For non-target data, outputs are reconstructed using the full-rank Δ component, defined as the residual between the original weights and the sum of target subcomponents; Δ captures all mechanisms never active on target data. 4. During training, Δ is adversarially ablated (0–100% of parameters) on target batches to force target outputs to be reconstructed solely from the sparse subcomponents; Δ is always enabled on non-target batches. 5. Both inactive subcomponents and Δ are adversarially ablated to ensure mechanistic faithfulness — model outputs remain unchanged for any hybrid of original and ablated weight matrices.1c:T8d2,方法核心是把 memory management 写成一个顺序决策问题。论文定义了一个 Memory MDP：状态同时编码任务进展和记忆状态，前者包括 goal type、当前 step phase、是否卡住、访问过的位置等，后者包括 memory size、是否已有可复用 plan、当前处于学习早期还是后期等。动作不是单一“retrieve”，而是一组带参数的 memory operation，包括 Retrieve、PlanInject、Re-Retrieve、Consolidate、Forget、NoOp，以及对应的 top_k、insight_k、graph hop 等参数选择。

控制策略没有用神经网络，也没有再训练一个小模型，而是用了 tabular contextual bandit + UCB exploration。这样做的原因很明确：一是在线部署时样本很少，二是状态特征是人工可解释的离散上下文，三是希望毫秒级决策而不是引入新的推理链路。策略从 human-readable priors warm start，而不是冷启动随机探索；任务结束后用 binary success 做反向折扣式 credit assignment，把一次任务成功或失败分摊给先前做过的 memory decisions。这个设计不追求严格的长期 credit 最优，而是追求在几十个任务内就能学到比固定启发式更稳的控制。

论文还强调它是 backend-agnostic wrapper。也就是说，MemCon 不定义新的存储格式，而是在 retrieve/store 抽象接口上做拦截和调度，因此可以套在 flat vector store、skill library、trajectory summary、graph memory、latent-token memory 等不同后端上。这个抽象是方法里最干净的部分，因为它把“存什么”和“怎么调用”分开了。

另外两个增强操作值得注意。PlanInject 面向重复 goal type，不再只做相似轨迹检索，而是优先注入抽象化、对象泛化后的成功计划模板；Re-Retrieve 面向 stuck 状态，当 agent 连续重复动作或无进展时，不是重复读同一批 top-k，而是换查询重检索。Consolidate 和 Forget 则处理长任务流中的记忆膨胀问题，避免 memory store 规模增长后把噪声一并带进 prompt。相比 MemGPT 这类让 LLM 自己当控制器的方案，MemCon 的区别不在记忆表达，而在把控制逻辑外置成一个廉价、可持续学习的策略层。1d:T959,The method formulates memory management as a sequential decision problem. The paper defines a Memory MDP whose state combines task-progress signals and memory-status signals. Task-side features include goal type, step phase, whether the agent is stuck, and visited locations. Memory-side features include memory size, whether reusable plans already exist, and the current learning phase. Actions are not limited to a generic retrieve call. The action space includes Retrieve, PlanInject, Re-Retrieve, Consolidate, Forget, and NoOp, together with operation parameters such as top_k, insight_k, and graph-hop depth.

The policy is intentionally simple: a tabular contextual bandit with UCB exploration. The choice is pragmatic. Online deployment offers limited samples, the context variables are interpretable and mostly discrete, and the controller must run in milliseconds. The policy is warm-started with human-readable priors instead of random exploration. After each task, binary task success is propagated backward through a reverse-discounted credit assignment scheme so earlier memory decisions receive partial credit or blame. This is not trying to solve full long-horizon RL optimally; it is trying to learn a useful controller within tens of tasks.

A key design point is backend agnosticism. MemCon does not define a new storage format. It wraps the abstract retrieve/store interface and decides how the backend should be called. That means the same controller can sit on top of flat vector stores, skill libraries, trajectory summaries, graph memories, and latent-token memories. This separation between what is stored and how it is accessed is one of the cleanest aspects of the paper.

Two augmented operations are especially relevant. PlanInject is used for recurring goal types: instead of nearest-neighbor retrieval over raw trajectories, it injects a distilled and object-generalized plan template from prior successful episodes. Re-Retrieve is triggered when the agent appears stuck, so the system issues an alternative query rather than re-reading the same failed top-k results. Consolidate and Forget address memory growth over long task streams, aiming to prevent prompt dilution and retrieval noise. Compared with LLM-as-controller approaches, the novelty is not in memory representation but in externalizing control into a cheap, persistent, online-learned policy layer.1e:T7ae,The evaluation is broad: 6 benchmarks, 3 agent frameworks, 3 LLM backbones, and 9 memory baselines. The environments named in the paper include ALFWorld, PDDL planning, ScienceWorld, TriviaQA, WebWalkerQA, and GAIA. The frameworks include Lobster, LangGraph, and Microsoft Agent Framework. The backbones include GPT-4.1-mini, Claude Sonnet-4, and DeepSeek-V3.2.

The main result is that MemCon achieves the best or near-best task success in most settings while also reducing average token usage by 5% to 20%. A representative number highlighted early in the paper is 67.9% success on ALFWorld with GPT-4.1-mini under the Lobster framework, the top score among the 10 memory systems compared in that cell. Relative to a no-memory baseline, the gains are often in the 5 to 30+ point range. That matters because the improvement appears across different backends, suggesting the controller itself contributes meaningful value.

The ablations are more important than the headline table. The paper isolates the contribution of the learned controller from the contribution of simply adding more memory operations. The reported pattern is that both matter, but the learned policy is a major source of gains, especially in three regimes: retrieving less in early tasks, preferring plan reuse for recurring goals, and triggering alternative retrieval when the agent is stuck. Another notable result is convergence speed: the controller learns useful behavior within tens of tasks, without any pretraining. That aligns with the choice of a contextual bandit rather than a heavier RL setup.

In terms of evidence quality, the strengths are breadth and backend diversity. The main limitation is the reward signal: task success is binary and coarse. For long-horizon tasks, the true value of individual memory actions may be obscured by final success or failure. So the paper demonstrates that lightweight online control is useful, not that the learned policy is close to 都有参考意义。","technical_approach":"Agora 围绕三个中心化训练没有的约束重构系统：（1）模型分片以 pipeline parallelism 为主，配合 subspace networks 把跨 stage 传递的激活压缩两个数量级，让互联网带宽下的 PP 可行；（2）数据并行副本之间不做每步同步的 all-reduce，而是采用稀疏、异步的参数平均（sparse 改动——异步/部分参与下学习率、batch、warmup 等需要怎么调才能保证收敛；Section 5–6 的实证——Pluralis-8B 的 loss 曲线、1B 消融里 85% 参与率和 10:1 异构容忍度这两个关键数字。若关心去中心化训练的开放问题，可进一步思考：异步+稀疏平均是否会隐式损害后期推理能力（尤其是 post-train 后的 benchmark 表现），以及 UPM 的“不可提取”在密码学/系统层面的实际强度。预计阅读时间 45–60 分钟。","authors_note":"Pluralis Research。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Frontier LLM pretraining is currently locked inside a small number of centralized datacenters because standard distributed training assumes co-located, homogeneous, highly reliable accelerators connected by a fast fabric. This makes the enormous pool of consumer-grade GPUs scattered across the internet — heterogeneous, behind commodity links, and free to join or leave at any time — unusable for training. The paper asks whether a meaningfully large LLM can be pretrained in a permissionless way over the public internet, by thousands of unreliable, heterogeneous, churn-prone participants, while achieving convergence and efficiency close to a centralized run.","importance":"This is a notable empirical milestone for decentralized training and pretraining systems research: it is not a simulation or toy proof of concept, but an actual public pretraining run at 8.6B parameters on 500B tokens. Its direct impact on mainstream pretraining recipes is limited, since the ML-side changes (asynchronous sparse averaging, partial participation, etc.) alter optimization dynamics and are validated primarily at the level of training-loss tracking rather than post-training downstream capability. Nonetheless, it cleanly identifies which standard assumptions must break under weak networking, heavy churn, and high heterogeneity (per-step synchronous all-reduce, fixed parallel topology, always-on nodes), which is informative for elastic cross-datacenter training and future crowdsourced / federated pretraining settings.","technical_approach":"$4","experiments":"The authors report Pluralis-8B: an 8.6B-parameter model trained on 500B FineWeb-Edu tokens over 40 days, with 330 contributor nodes (mostly consumer GPUs plus a small set of operator nodes), experiencing 669 joins and 607 departures during the run. Training loss closely tracks a centralized reference throughout. 1B-scale ablations show convergence tolerates compute heterogeneity up to 10:1; degradation appears only when all-reduce participation drops below roughly 85%, with full recovery once failing nodes are pruned. Sustained throughput is about 170k tokens/s, or 4.2 tokens/TFLOP — roughly 63% of the efficiency of a centralized H100 baseline — maintained under continuous churn.","reading_guide":"Skim, but treat it as a serious systems technical report. Focus on three parts: Section 3's system design — why pipeline parallelism is favored over tensor parallelism / FSDP under internet conditions, how subspace networks compress cross-stage communication, and what exactly sparse asynchronous parameter averaging synchronizes and at what frequency; Section 4's ML-side changes — how LR, batch, warmup, and related knobs must be adjusted to keep async, partially-participated training stable; Sections 5–6's empirical results — in particular the loss-tracking curve for Pluralis-8B and the two ablation thresholds: ~85% participation and 10:1 heterogeneity tolerance. For open questions, note that asynchronous sparse averaging may have subtle downstream-quality effects not visible in training loss alone, and that the UPM claim of unextractability deserves scrutiny at both the cryptographic and systems level. Its empirical success has motivated a growing body of theoretical work that interprets Muon as steepest descent under the spectral norm. Yet it remains unclear which of Muon's advantages stem from its update rule itself and which are artifacts of the scale, archi","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-14T20:21:38Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2607_13246.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“Muon 在大模型上有没有用”，而是更基础的问题：把架构、数据、规模这些混杂因素拿掉之后，Muon 这个优化器本身到底在什么问题上占优。作者选了低秩矩阵分解、矩阵补全和非负矩阵分解这类结构清楚、谱性质明确的问题，专门检验 Muon 的正交化更新是否真的能利用谱结构、改善病态条件下的优化，还是它在 LLM 里的收益更多来自深网特有的训练动力学。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义在于，它给 Muon 这类“矩阵感知”优化器降了一次噪。过去不少结论来自 GPT 类训练，容易把优化器效果和模型深度、归一化、数据分布、batch 规模混在一起。这里的结果比较克制：在标准低秩分解和矩阵补全上，只要调参公平，AdamW 和 GD 往往能追平甚至超过 Muon；Muon 的稳定优势主要出现在 NMF 这类存在冗余因子竞争、表示多样性更关键的约束问题上。对做 LLM 预训练的人，这意味着 Muon 不是一个“普适更优”的替代品，它更像是在某些几何结构下有偏好的更新规则。这个判断比单纯看大模型 loss 曲线更有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者没有提出新优化器，而是做了一个控制变量很严的再评估。核心做法有三层。第一，选取可系统扫描的问题族：对称 PSD 低秩分解、一般矩阵补全，以及非负矩阵分解。它们分别覆盖了无约束低秩恢复、观测缺失、以及带非负约束和因子冗余的情形。第二，做等预算、细调参比较。论文强调 Muon、Adam、AdamW、GD 都经过系统调参，而不是沿用社区默认超参，这一点很关键，因为 Muon 的 reported gain 很容易受学习率、动量、weight decay、正交化近似步数影响。第三，围绕“谱结构是否真的被利用”来解释结果。Muon 的更新会对梯度矩阵做近似正交化，相当于压平奇异值方向上的尺度差异，使更新更接近谱范数意义下的最速下降。直觉上这应当有利于低秩问题，但作者发现，在经典矩阵分解里，这种谱归一化并不天然优于坐标自适应；相反，AdamW 的逐坐标缩放在病态条件数下更直接。Muon 真正显出优势的地方，是 NMF 中它会抑制多个因子朝同一方向塌缩，减少冗余表示，促使因子更分散、更“去共线”。也就是说，Muon 的收益未必来自更快地拟合主奇异方向，而可能来自一种隐式的表示正则化。这个解释和它在深网里可能改善特征多样性、缓解表示退化的经验现象是相通的。","experiments":"从全文给出的结论看，实验重点不是追求单一最好数值，而是比较不同问题结构下的相对排序。主要发现有四点。第一，在标准低秩矩阵分解上，Muon 的优势在公平调参后基本消失，AdamW 和 GD 可以达到相当甚至更好的收敛速度与最终误差。第二，在矩阵补全这类带采样缺失的问题上，Muon 同样没有表现出稳定领先，说明“梯度正交化自动更适合谱问题”这个直觉并不成立。第三，在 NMF 上，Muon 有更一致的优势，尤其是在过参数化或因子冗余更明显的设定下，能得到更好的重构和更少的因子重合。第四，病态条件数并不会自动把优势推向 Muon；很多情况下 AdamW 的自适应缩放更有效。摘要里没有给出完整数字表，但正文的价值在于系统扫了条件数、参数化程度、任务变体，并把结论建立在 tuned comparison 上，而不是单点实验。对研究者来说，最该看的不是某个提升百分点，而是“哪类问题 Muon 不赢、哪类问题它才开始赢”这个边界。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 里的三个研究问题和 findings，再看实验主表与不同任务设定的对比图，最后补 Appendix 中 Muon 机制说明和 bilinear / completion 变体。预计 20 到 30 分钟能抓住主线。阅读时重点留意两件事：一是作者如何定义“公平比较”，包括超参搜索和预算控制；二是 NMF 里对因子冗余、表示多样性的解释，这部分比单纯的 loss 排名更有启发。如果你关心 Muon 能否迁移到 LLM 预训练 recipe，值得把这篇和大规模 Muon 经验论文对照着看：这里提供的是失败案例和适用边界。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a cleaner question than “does Muon help on large models?”: once architecture, data, and scale confounders are removed, where does Muon itself actually help. The authors use low-rank matrix factorization, matrix completion, and nonnegative matrix factorization as controlled testbeds to examine whether Muon’s orthogonalized update truly exploits spectral structure and improves optimization under ill-conditioning, or whether its gains in LLM training are tied to deep-network-specific dynamics.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is that it deconfounds a popular optimizer. Much of the excitement around Muon comes from GPT-style training, where optimizer effects are entangled with depth, normalization, data mixture, and large-batch behavior. The conclusion here is measured: on standard low-rank factorization and matrix completion, Muon’s reported gains largely disappear under fair tuning, and AdamW or even GD can match or beat it; Muon shows a more consistent advantage mainly in NMF-like settings where redundant factors compete and representation diversity matters. For LLM pretraining, that means Muon should not be read as a uniformly better drop-in replacement. Model Size, Search, Learning, and Feedback","abstract":"Reinforcement Learning (RL) post-training is increasingly used to adapt foundation models for reasoning, planning, and feedback-driven robot-learning pipelines, but constrained post-training resources are often summarized by a single total FLOP budget. int","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-15T02:34:35Z","category_id":"scaling","cover_url":"/covers/2607_13389.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作研究 质量。贡献量级不是提出一个单一训练配方，而是提供一套算力会计和条件 frontier 的分析框架。它适合用来设计 reasoning / verifier / reward model 后训练实验，避免把不同 reward 系统或不同模型尺寸的结果放在“同 FLOPs”标签下直接比较。","technical_approach":"方法核心是把 GRPO reward、verifier 或 process reward model 的推理成本。主要控制变量是 update fraction ρ，即 C_learning / C_total。小 ρ 表示算力更多用于 rollout/search 或 reward evaluation；大 ρ 表示更多算力用于 policy update。这个分解的关键区别在于，它不把 reward model 推理成本当作免费项。rule-based reward 的额外成本很低，而 PRM 或 learned verifier 会占用明显预算；如果忽略这部分，PRM 方案会看起来像是在同样预算下拥有更多有效训练资源。实验设置使用 LoRA-adapted Qwen2.5 policy，在数学推理任务上做 GRPO，比较不同模型尺寸、不同 ρ、不同 reward 系统和不同 evaluation fixed-budget allocation in RL post-training. Given the same post-training FLOP budget, it asks whether compute should be spent on a larger policy, more rollout search, more policy updates, or stronger but more expensive reward feedback. The central issue is that total FLOPs alone under-specify an RL run: two runs with the same budget can differ mechanistically in how much exploration, learning, and reward evaluation they buy.","importance":"Its main value for foundation-model training is a clean accounting lens for the pretrain-to-posttrain interface. Unlike continuous diffusion, where the state space is fixed, DDMs are fundamentally shaped by how the discrete state space is constructed: the tokenization scheme, the vocabulary topology, and domain-specific structural alphabets. This work introduces a unified conceptual framewor","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-15T04:23:22Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2607_13431.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作试图统一 byte、VQ 图像 token、codec audio token、video token，也可以是氨基酸、核苷酸、原子和键类型等自然 alphabet。在这个视角下，已有方法可被放进同一设计空间。D3PM 类方法显式定义 categorical transition matrix，允许 uniform、nearest-neighbor 或结构感知替换。Masking/absorbing-state 方法把 mask token 作为吸收态，训练模型从部分损坏序列恢复 clean token；这与 masked LM 接近，但采样是多步迭代而不是一次性填空。Score-或 ratio-based 方法不直接预测连续 score，而是在离散状态上学习概率比或去噪方向，代表路线包括 SEDD、MDLM、LLaDA construction of the discrete state space. The central question is not only which denoising loss to use, but how tokenization, vocabulary topology, and domain-specific alphabets shape the corruption process, the reverse model, the sampling order, and the attainable generation behavior.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model pretraining is the shift from viewing diffusion language models as merely non-autoregressive decoders to viewing them as a joint design problem over tokenization, state-space geometry, and denoising dynamics. For unified multimodal modeling, the point is even sharper: image, audio, and video tokens produced by VQ models, residual quantizers, or codecs have codebook structure, and a corruption process that ignores this structure treats all substitutions as equally meaningful. This reuse changes the residual-scaling problem: in an untied Transformer, each residual branch receives and applies its own parameter update, whereas in a looped Transformer one shared update aggregates gradients from repeated visits and is read back by those same visits in the next linearized forward pass. compute、recurrent depth、looped block、reasoning-time unrolling 继续发展，这类 tied-weight 稳定性分析会直接影响预训练 recipe。论文的贡献量级不在于提出全新架构，而在于补上了一个此前分析缺失但工程上真实存在的缩放规则。","technical_approach":"技术核心是把共享层重复访问带来的额外放大项显式写进一阶扰动分析。作者从 DeepNorm 的思路出发：在 Post-LN Transformer 中，通过设置 skip scale α 和残差分支初始化增益 β，控制参数更新对前向表示的累计影响。DeepNorm 在 untied depth 下得到的稳定条件大致是 M(β/α)^2=O(1)，其中 M=2N 是残差子层访问次数。问题在于 looped Transformer 中，同一物理子层会被访问 R 次，梯度写入和参数读取都发生了跨轮共享。作者引入 visit-alignment coefficient κ_R 来刻画不同 round 的梯度与敏感度是否对齐。如果不同 visit 近似去相关，κ_R 不大，系统退化回 DeepNorm 的情形；如果不同 visit 高度对齐，κ_R 会随 R 增长，导致共享更新的影响被额外放大。由此得到 loop-aware 的稳定条件 κ_R M(β/α)^2=O(1)。在参数化族 α=(cN)^p、β=(dN)^(-p) 下，若 visit decorrelate，则仍恢复 p=1/4；若处于保守的 aligned regime，固定物理深度 K、增加循环次数 R 时，需要把阈值提高到 p=1/2。基于这个推导，作者提出 DeepLoop：沿用 DeepNorm 的 Post-LN 结构，但把缩放改成更保守的 α=(2N)^(1/2)、β=(8N)^(-1/2)。它不是新模块，也不引入门控、辅助损失或学习到的残差系数，只是一条针对 tied-depth 的初始化/缩放规则。这个设计的优点是简单，代价是它偏保守，尤其在 visit 实际并不强对齐时，可能不是最优但更稳。","experiments":"实验是 GPT-style 语言模型上的控制预训练消融，规模覆盖 GPT-2 small 和 GPT-2 medium。核心结果与理论预测一致。第一，在 R=1 时，也就是没有真正循环复用时，DeepLoop 基本中性，不会明显优于或劣于 DeepNorm，这说明它没有靠额外技巧“刷分”。第二，随着 loop count 增大，DeepLoop 在验证 loss 上更稳定地优于原始 DeepNorm，说明 tied-depth 的修正确实在起作用。第三，这个收益能迁移到一个八任务语言模型评测套件：R=1 时两者接近，R>1 后结果逐步偏向 DeepLoop。论文摘要没有给出具体百分点，但从全文叙述看，作者更强调趋势的条件依赖性：只有当共享层被重复访问时，新的缩放规则才开始显著有用。对做预训练的人，这比单个 benchmark 数字更重要，因为它说明收益来自正确建模 looped depth，而不是偶然的超参偏置。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是按“问题定义—理论推导—控制实验”三步看。先读 Introduction，把 tied-depth effect 和 κ_R 的直觉弄清楚；再看理论部分里从 DeepNorm 条件推到 loop-aware 条件的关键公式，重点理解为什么会多出一个 visit alignment 因子；最后看 GPT-2 small/medium 的预训练曲线和下游八任务结果。预计 25 到 35 分钟。阅读时最值得留意的是两点：一是 κ_R 不是拍脑袋加的常数，而是把共享参数的 write/read 双重聚合写进了一阶分析；二是 DeepLoop 选择保守的 p=1/2，不是因为它总是最优，而是因为在固定 K、增大 R 的 worst-case aligned regime 下更稳。如果你在关注 recurrent depth、test-time compute 或 parameter-efficient depth scaling，这篇值得记住它的缩放逻辑。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a stability gap in looped Transformers. When a small set of physical blocks is reused for many rounds, effective depth increases, but the residual-branch parameters are shared across visits. That means the same parameter tensor accumulates gradients from multiple visits and is then read back by those same visits in the next linearized forward pass. DeepNorm was derived for untied depth, where each layer owns its own parameters, so it does not directly apply here. The core question is: how should residual scaling be set in tied-depth Transformers so that training remains first-order stable as unrolled depth grows?","importance":"For pretraining research, the paper matters for two reasons. First, it treats depth scaling separately from parameter scaling, which is increasingly relevant as people explore recurrent depth, looped blocks, and test-time-compute-heavy models. Second, it shows that residual parameterization should depend not only on nominal depth N but also on how that depth is realized. If future language models rely more on repeated computation with shared weights, this kind of tied-weight stability analysis becomes part of the pretraining recipe. The contribution is not a new architecture class, but a missing scaling rule for a realistic regime.","technical_approach":"$b","experiments":"The experiments are controlled GPT-style language-model pretraining ablations at GPT-2 small and GPT-2 medium scale. The main findings match the theory. First, at R = 1, where there is no actual revisiting of shared blocks, DeepLoop is essentially neutral relative to DeepNorm, which is a useful sanity check. Second, as the loop count increases, DeepLoop consistently improves final validation loss over the original DeepNorm parameterization, indicating that the tied-depth correction matters exactly when reuse becomes substantial. Third, the gain transfers to an eight-task language-model evaluation suite: results are close at R = 1 and increasingly favor DeepLoop as recurrent depth grows. The abstract does not provide detailed numbers, but the full paper emphasizes conditional trends rather than a single benchmark headline. That is the right emphasis here: the benefit appears when depth is realized through repeated visits to shared parameters.","reading_guide":"A good skim is to read it in three passes: problem setup, theory, then controlled experiments. Start with the Introduction to internalize the tied-depth effect and the intuition behind kappa_R. Then read the derivation that modifies the DeepNorm condition and understand why shared parameters create an extra alignment factor. Finally, inspect the GPT-2 small/medium training curves and the downstream eight-task results. About 25–35 minutes should be enough. Two details are worth focusing on: kappa_R is not an ad hoc constant but a way to capture the double aggregation induced by shared writes and shared reads; and the choice p = 1/2 is intentionally conservative, aimed at the fixed-K, growing-R aligned regime rather than claiming universal optimality. If you care about recurrent depth, test-time compute, or parameter-efficient depth scaling, the scaling logic is the main takeaway.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"为 Looped Transformer 提供了坚实的初始化理论（DeepNorm 的变体），对探索 test-time compute 或参数复用架构的研究员有直接启发，重点看缩放系数的推导。","en":"Provides a solid initialization theory (a DeepNorm variant) for Looped Transformers, highly relevant for researchers exploring test-time compute or parameter-reuse architectures; focus on the derivation of the scaling coefficients."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Princeton University"],"authors":["Shuzhen Li","Yifan Zhang","Jiacheng Guo","Quanquan Gu","Mengdi Wang"],"notable_authors":["Mengdi Wang","Quanquan Gu"],"tags":["Looped Transformer","Depth Scaling","Initialization","DeepNorm","Parameter Efficiency"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13491","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.13491","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}}]},{"id":"efficient","emoji":"⚡","name":"高效推理与架构","description":"KV-Cache优化、量化/剪枝/蒸馏、推测解码、注意力优化、长上下文推理、模型压缩、推理系统/Serving","papers":[{"id":"2607.13205","title":"Adaptive Filtering of the KV Cache: Diagnosing and Correcting Structural-Role Bias in LLM Inference","abstract":"Attention-based KV cache eviction (H2O and its descendants) compresses the memory-constrained state of a long-context model by ranking tokens on accumulated attention mass, treated here as signal energy, and keeping the heaviest. attention-based KV-cache eviction. Methods such as H2O keep tokens with the largest accumulated attention mass, but on schema-dense contexts such as nested JSON, XML, and markdown tables, this criterion over-keeps structural tokens and schema keys while evicting answer-bearing values.","importance":"The contribution is mainly in efficient inference, but it has clear implications for long-context model evaluation and deployment. The key lesson is that attention mass is not always a reliable proxy for information value. In structured inputs, delimiters, whitespace, and keys can behave as distributed attention sinks. This matters for RAG, tool traces, agent logs, and structured-data reasoning, where cache compression can erase the actual answer even when the model’s full-cache behavior is correct. It also argues for adding schema-dense inputs to KV-compression and long-context benchmarks.","technical_approach":"The authors frame KV-cache eviction as an adaptive filtering problem under a hard memory budget. H2O treats accumulated attention mass as signal energy and retains heavy hitters. The paper shows that, in structured text, this filter locks onto high-energy but low-content roles. Tokens are labeled by structural role, including KEY, VALUE, DELIM, HEADER, PROSE, and whitespace. The authors measure both attention mass and retention rate by role, then run counterfactual eviction experiments that ban one role from the candidate set to test causal importance. The proposed fix is a role-conditional allocator layered on SnapKV. SnapKV uses a recent-query attention window plus 1-D max pooling over the KV axis; this already suppresses isolated sink tokens and favors content clusters. The role allocator adds explicit caps for noisy roles and floor protection for content roles. A small linear role probe, about 15 MB, is evaluated as a parser substitute for deployment.","experiments":"$c","reading_guide":"Skim in 35–50 minutes. 的代理，但这个假设未被严格检验。论文的核心任务就是把“答对了”与“确实依赖视频内容才答对”这两件事拆开，并给出可统计检验的诊断量。","importance":"从基座研究视角看，这篇工作的重要性不在于提出新模型，而在于它直接挑战了多模态预训练和后训练里一个常见但危险的默认前提：benchmark 提升就等于感知能力提升。若这个前提不成立，很多架构选择、数据配比、视频 token 设计、甚至对 temporal module 的投入，都可能是在优化语言侧捷径而不是视觉建模本身。对 VLM/video LM 研究者，这篇论文的价值主要有三点。第一，它把 accuracy 与 visual dependency 明确区分成两条轴，而不是笼统说“有些题可以不看视频”。第二，它给出 per-question 的诊断指标 VDG，可直接用于数据筛选、benchmark VDG。对同一道题，在原始视频条件和 black-screen 条件下分别作答，VDG 定义为两种条件下正确性的差值，粒度是 per-question，而不是只看整体均值。这样做的好处是可以把题目按“是否真正依赖视觉输入”分层，也能比较不同模型在同一题上的依赖差异。作者进一步用 paired McNemar test 去证明 accuracy 与 visual dependency 是统计上可分离的：如果两个模型在原视频上准确率显著不同，但在黑屏条件下无显著差异，就说明更高 accuracy 并不自动意味着更强视觉依赖。除了黑屏对照，论文还设计了一个四级诊断 ladder：black screen、single frame、shuffled frames、original video。这个设计很聪明，因为它把视频信息拆成几种成分：有没有任何视觉内容、单帧空间信息是否足够、帧多样性是否重要、时间顺序是否重要。再配合 FPS 从 0.5 到 24 的消融，可以更细地看 benchmark 到底在奖励什么。作者还分析了压缩鲁棒性，用 CRF 曲线解释为什么一些模型在视频压缩下看起来“很稳”，其实可能只是 benchmark 中大量低视觉依赖题目把真正敏感题目的退化抵消了。实验覆盖 20 个模型、10 个架构家族、2B 到 78B，包括开源权重和 API 模型；主分析模型包括 Qwen2-VL-7B、LLaVA-Video-7B、InternVL2-8B，另外还有 Qwen2.5/3-VL、InternVL2/2.5、VideoLLaMA2 等。作者对 4-bit 量化结果专门加了 caveat，没有把它们用于强 dependency 可以分离。第二，任务类型的 VDG 排名在 20 个模型上相当稳定：Attribute Perception 的视觉依赖最高，平均约 0.41；Temporal Reasoning 接近语言基线，约 0.06。这不是单模型偶然现象。第三，这个 task-type taxonomy 在不同 benchmark 上有一致性。作者称 Video-MME 的标签可以 100% 预测 MVBench 的分类（7/7），并且能泛化到 EgoSchema，说明“哪些题真依赖视觉”具有跨 benchmark 稳定性。第四，四条件 ladder 显示 temporal ordering 对准确率贡献几乎为零，而 frame diversity 才是主要来源；也就是说，打乱帧顺序往往不怎么伤性能，但退化成单帧会更明显。作者还指出 Qwen3-VL 相比 Qwen2.5-VL 出现 EgoSchema；模型规模覆盖 2B–78B。论文还区分了全精度与 4-bit 量化两档，避免把量化噪声误当成架构结论。","reading_guide":"这篇适合精读，重点不在模型细节，而在评测设计和统计论证。先看引言和 VDG 定义，确认作者到底在测“视觉依赖”而不是“视觉能力”的某个模糊替代物。然后直接读 Section 4.2 的 McNemar dissociation，这是全文最关键的证据链。接着看 task-type VDG spectrum 和 cross-benchmark consistency，理解哪些题型天然更容易被语言先验解掉。之后读 Section 5.5 的四条件 ladder，再结合 FPS ablation，看作者如何把视频信息拆成 spatial、diversity、temporal 三部分。压缩鲁棒性分析也值得看，因为它提醒人不要把 flat curve 误读成模型稳健。若做视频预训练或视频 benchmark，建议重点盯住图表里 black screen、single frame、shuffled frame 与 original video 的差值，而不是只看原始 accuracy 排名。阅读时长大约 45–60 分钟；若只抓主线，先看 VDG 定义、McNemar 表、task-type 排名图、四条件诊断图即可。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a foundational evaluation problem rather than a new modeling trick: when a video LLM scores well on a benchmark, how much of that accuracy actually depends on visual evidence, and how much comes from language priors, question templates, and dataset bias? Current leaderboards implicitly treat benchmark accuracy as a proxy for visual understanding. This work tests that assumption directly and asks whether correctness and visual dependency are in fact separable.","importance":"Its importance lies in evaluation hygiene for multimodal pretraining. If benchmark gains do not reliably track visual dependency, then architecture choices, data mixtures, temporal modules, and video tokenization strategies may be optimized against shortcuts rather than genuine video understanding. Second, it introduces a per-question diagnostic, VDG, that can be used for benchmark auditing, data filtering, and post-training analysis. Existing evaluations cannot separate these two failures; their reference policies either read privileged information the agent never sees, or are missing altogether. We introduce STOCKTAKE, a 26-week supply-","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-15T09:08:27Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2607_13618.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“LLM agent 在长周期决策里表现差”这个泛问题，而是更细的一层：在部分可观测、长期累积成本的任务里，失败到底来自没看懂世界，还是看懂了却没把判断落实成动作。现有 supply-chain / retail 一类 benchmark 往往只给最终成本，或者拿能看到特权信息的 oracle 当上界，因此把 state estimation failure 和 control failure 混在一起，无法定位 knowing-doing gap。STOCKTAKE 的核心设计，是构造一个可以计算“公平 oracle”的长周期 POMDP 基准，让参考策略和 agent 看到完全相同的观测流，从而把感知与行动两类失败拆开测。","importance":"对基座研究的直接价值不在供应链任务本身，而在评测方法：它给 agent 评测补上了一个长期缺口——如何在部分可观测环境里做可归因的能力分解。这个问题和 LLM pretrain/post-train 都有关。若一个模型能在 rationale 里稳定识别隐变量，却在动作上持续失配，问题更可能出在 policy elicitation、训练目标、工具调用接口或长程决策校准，而不是世界模型本身。反过来，如果 detection lag 很长，则更像是表征、记忆、证据整合或训练数据覆盖的问题。论文的贡献量级主要体现在 benchmark 设计和评测归因框架，而不是提出新的 agent 算法。","technical_approach":"方法上有三个关键点。第一，任务被形式化为一个 26 周的 factored POMDP。环境里有六个隐藏因子过程，驱动需求、供应、物流、质量等风险；agent 每周只能看到仪表盘症状，如库存、在途订单、需求实现、运价、港口等待、供应商评分和质检报告，然后做补货决策。第二，作者刻意把隐藏结构做成 factorized，并固定 event tape，因此可以对每个隐藏因子运行 exact Bayes filter，再用 rollout policy 在相同观测条件下做决策，得到 fair oracle。这里“公平”的关键不是 oracle 强，而是它不读 agent 看不到的真值；论文也明确说明只有一处披露的不对称。第三，围绕这个 fair oracle 定义了一组分解指标，而不是只看总成本。skill score 把 agent 定位在 symptom-blind base-stock floor 和 fair Bayesian oracle 之间；detection lag 衡量 agent 从隐藏事件发生到在 rationale 中点名该因子的延迟；knowing-doing rate 则统计已经正确诊断的压力周里，动作仍导致 stockout 或过度保护成本的比例。作者还设计了 isolated / persistent / compound 三类 stress profile，在世界模型不变的前提下控制隐藏故障的重叠方式，用来观察感知和行动错误如何耦合。全文里一个值得注意的细节是，benchmark 不给策略 playbook，只给 rules-only prompt，这样更接近“模型自身把诊断转成动作”的能力，而不是提示工程后的流程执行。","experiments":"实验覆盖 50 个 seed、200 个 episode，按 stress profile 分组评估 Claude Sonnet 5、GPT-5.4、DeepSeek-V4-Pro、Grok 4.5 四个模型。最关键的结果有两点。第一，感知层面的差异并不大：四个模型都能识别 84%–88% 的隐藏因子 episode，通常在事件发生后一周内就能在 rationale 中点名，甚至 skill score 较低的模型有时识别更快。第二，成本层面的差异很大，而且与 detection 不同步：四个模型里有两个整体表现低于 symptom-blind floor，也就是在大约一半的 seed 上，“完全不读症状、按固定库存规则下单”反而更便宜。这说明错误主要不是没看见，而是看见后动作没有跟上。persistent seeds 上，所有模型在“已正确诊断”的压力周里，仍有 34%–43% 的周最终发生 stockout。作者进一步分析指出，这里面部分是 severity confound：越严重的周越容易被诊断出来，也越容易把货架压穿；但即便考虑这一点，模型之间真正拉开差距的仍是从 diagnosis 到 order quantity / route / supplier / protection action 的映射质量。论文还讨论了 knowing-doing gap 的双向性：不仅会订少、订晚，也会为了防风险而付出过高保护成本，比如过度锁运价、过度空运或过度检查。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三处。第一看任务定义和 Figure 1，弄清每周观测、可选动作、成本项和隐藏因子分别是什么；如果这里没看明白，后面的指标会显得抽象。第二看 fair oracle 的构造部分，重点是 exact Bayes filter per factor 和 rollout policy 如何共享同一 observation stream，这决定了 benchmark 是否真的能把 knowing 与 doing 分开。第三看结果章节里按 stress profile 分解的表和图，尤其是 detection lag、skill score、knowing-doing rate 三者的错位关系。预计 25–35 分钟能把主要思想读清。若想进一步用到自己的 agent 评测里，建议再细看 Section 4–5 中关于公平性假设、唯一信息不对称、severity 分析和 rationale-based metric 的定义边界，因为这些地方决定了指标是否会被 prompt style 或 verbalization 能力污染。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not merely about poor long-horizon agent performance. It targets a more specific identification problem: in partially observed, cost-accumulating decision tasks, did the agent fail because it inferred the hidden state incorrectly, or because it inferred it correctly and still failed to act on that belief? Existing supply-chain and retail benchmarks usually report only final cost, or compare against privileged oracles that observe latent state unavailable to the agent. That setup entangles state-estimation failure with control failure. STOCKTAKE is designed to make a fair oracle computable, so the benchmark can separate perception from action and measure the knowing-doing gap directly.","importance":"Its main value for foundation-model research is methodological rather than domain-specific. If detection itself is slow or inconsistent, the issue looks more like representation quality, memory, evidence integration, or data coverage. Final-answer math scores are a weak signal for model development because they conflate reasoning, memorization, formatting, and exploitation of surface cues. A benchmark built around symbolic variants and internal mechanisms could provide a sharper diagnostic for contamination, template dependence, and reasoning generalization. It may also help separate post-training methods that merely improve answer accuracy from those that create more stable reasoning behavior. The impact depends heavily on the final scoring protocol, model access, and annotation quality; the current document is a competition proposal, not an empirical study.","technical_approach":"The design combines olympiad-level problems, symbolic reasoning annotations, and access to frontier reasoning models. The problem pool includes 180 tasks from AIMO 2, AIMO 3, JMO, and recent AIME sets, with half of both train and test problems claimed to be newly published to reduce internet leakage. The organizers annotate symbolic reasoning chains that capture logical dependencies in valid solutions. These chains are then used to generate controlled adversarial variants, in the spirit of GSM-Symbolic but at a much harder mathematical level. Participants are expected to infer robustness from behavior and internal representations. This differs from benchmarks such as RAVEL, InterpBench, and MIB, which often test interpretability methods in synthetic or semi-synthetic settings with known factors or planted circuits. Here the target is whether interpretability tools can make useful judgments about real frontier models on difficult reasoning tasks.","experiments":"There are no completed experimental results. The concrete numbers are the planned 180-problem corpus, 50% newly published problems in both train and test splits, symbolic annotations for variant generation, and an expected 20–40 strong submissions. The proposal also cites more than 4,000 teams participating in AIMO 3 on Kaggle as evidence of community interest. Missing details include baseline interpretability methods, the exact internal-access interface, robustness-label construction, inter-annotator validation for symbolic chains, and the final metric. These details will determine whether the challenge becomes a reusable scientific instrument or mainly a competition event.","reading_guide":"Skim in 20–30 minutes. We report a case in which this generation step silently failed, and use it to argue that the failure mode is structural rather than incidental. In a multilingual (Turkish/English) faithfulness-judgment corpus, a decoding-budget parameter shared between judging and generation calls truncated one producer's hall","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-15T11:12:54Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2607_13707.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论的是 synthetic LLM-as-judge 语料构建里的一个很具体但很容易被忽视的问题：当研究者用一个 LLM 生成“错误答案/幻觉答案”作为 judge 的负样本时，如果生成环节本身悄悄退化，后续所有关于 judge 偏差的统计分析都可能建立在坏刺激上，而且常规的稳健性检查未必能发现。作者把这个问题上升为 test oracle problem：对于一条自动生成的负样本，是否存在便宜、机械、逐条可执行的正确性检查。如果没有，那么语料构建流程就天然缺少 item-level 审计能力。","importance":"对基座预训练研究，这不是直接改模型或训练配方的论文，但它和评测方法、合成数据质量、自动 judge 可靠性高度相关。现在很多 post-train、reward modeling、preference data、self-play 数据合成都依赖 LLM 生成中间产物，再用另一个模型打分或筛选。论文提醒的一点很重要：如果生成端没有可验证 oracle，后续再精细的统计显著性、复现实验、机制解释，都可能只是把上游数据缺陷包装得更可信。它的贡献不在提出复杂新算法，而在把一个常见实践问题讲清楚：LLM-generated negatives 和 mechanical perturbation negatives 在可审计性上并不对称。这个判断对做合成预训练数据、judge benchmark、reward 数据集的人都有直接参考价值。","technical_approach":"论文的方法部分其实是一种失败案例剖析加验证协议设计。作者在构建一个土耳其语/英语双语 faithfulness-judgment corpus 时，错误地让 judging call 和 generation call 共享同一个 decoding-budget 参数。这个预算对 judge 的单 token 输出足够，但对生成段落级 hallucinated answer 来说过小，结果把某个 producer 生成的土耳其语负样本截断成很短的几词片段。随后这些坏样本进入 judge 评测流程，制造出一个看起来很显著的跨语言偏差效应。\n\n论文的关键不是“发现了一个 bug”，而是系统说明为什么常规检查抓不到它。作者做了四类标准 aggregate-level robustness 检查，但它们都只看 judge 行为统计，不看刺激本身，因此对 item-level degeneration 天然失明。接着作者用 test oracle 的视角区分两类负样本构造：\n1. LLM-generated-negative design：负样本由模型自由生成，没有廉价 oracle 去判断一条样本是“合理的错误答案”还是“退化的短残片”。\n2. Mechanical-perturbation design：负样本由 gold answer 经过确定性变换得到，因此 gold 与 negative 之间存在可机械验证的关系，可以用字符串比较之类的方法做 item-level 检查。\n\n为了把这个论点从概念变成证据，作者做了一个 positive control：在一个最小化的、oracle-bearing 的 mechanical corpus 上注入与原故障最接近的机械类比错误，结果用 gold-to-negative string comparison 可以 100% 检出，且不需要额外 API 调用和人工阅读。对于无法避免使用 oracle-less 生成设计的场景，作者提出一个很朴素的 validation protocol：在做任何聚合统计前，按 generation condition 人工抽读 15 到 20 条原始生成样本，并报告长度分布与 degeneration rate。这个协议不花哨，但论文的论证是，至少在他们的故障类型上，这是唯一真正有效的检查。","experiments":"实验核心是一个 A/B 验证过的真实故障案例。坏数据会制造出一个看似稳健的现象：某个 judge 在土耳其语上的选择准确率相对英语出现 32 个点的坍塌，而且这个效应从 N=50 扩展到 N=500 都能复现。作者还给出了三层 mechanistic account，并做了 controlled producer-swap experiment，结果都支持这个“偏差故事”。但这些支持后来被证明是建立在退化刺激上的假象。\n\n更有信息量的是纠错后的分析。作者指出，生成故障会以两种不同方式污染结论：一种是凭空制造一个不存在的效应；另一种是把原本真实存在的效应扭曲成错误的大小或形态。aggregate metrics 无法区分这两类污染。也就是说，即便某个偏差现象并非完全虚构，上游刺激退化仍会改变你对它的定量判断。\n\npositive control 部分则展示了设计差异：在 mechanical-perturbation 语料上注入对应故障后，简单的字符串级 oracle 检查达到 100% 检出率，成本为零 API、零人工。这个结果不说明 mechanical design 在所有方面都更好，但至少说明它在可审计性上有明确优势。\n\n如果从实验局限看，论文没有去普查现有 judge corpora 有多少已经受影响，也没有给出通用阈值证明 15 到 20 条人工抽检在所有场景都足够。它更像一个经过充分验证的 failure analysis，而不是大规模基准研究。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议按问题链条来读。先看引言里对故障案例的叙述，弄清楚 fabricated effect 是怎么形成的；再看 Section 4.1 和 4.2，重点理解“消失、扭曲”这两类污染分别是什么意思；最后看 Section 4.3 的 positive control 和 Section 5 的 validation protocol。这样能最快抓住论文真正的贡献，不会把它误读成单纯的 pipeline 事故记录。\n\n如果时间有限，优先看故障复现图表、producer-swap 实验、以及纠错前后结果对比。预计 20 到 30 分钟可以完成一次有效泛读。值得特别留意的一点是，作者不是在泛泛批评 LLM-as-judge，而是在指出 synthetic corpus 构建里“负样本生成无 oracle”这一结构性缺口。做合成数据、reward 数据、judge benchmark 的人都应该把这点记下来。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a specific but underappreciated failure mode in synthetic LLM-as-judge corpora. Many judge-bias studies create negative examples by asking an LLM to generate a fluent but incorrect answer, then compare it against a factual answer. If that generation step silently degenerates, the downstream bias analysis can become invalid even when the aggregate statistics look strong. The paper reframes this as a test oracle problem: for each generated negative example, is there a cheap, mechanical way to verify that the item is valid? If not, the corpus construction pipeline lacks item-level auditability by design.","importance":"This is not a model-training paper, but it is highly relevant to evaluation methodology, synthetic data quality, and automatic judging. A large amount of post-training, preference collection, reward modeling, and synthetic supervision now depends on one model generating intermediate artifacts that another model scores or filters. The paper makes a useful point: if the generation side has no reliable oracle, then replication, significance testing, and even mechanistic explanations can all become downstream confirmations of bad stimuli. The contribution is not a new algorithm. It is a careful argument that LLM-generated negatives and mechanically perturbed negatives are not symmetric in auditability. That matters for anyone building judge benchmarks, synthetic training corpora, or reward datasets.","technical_approach":"$14","experiments":"$15","reading_guide":"For a skim, follow the causal chain. Existing consistency regularizers often impose global constraints over image and text embeddings, but web image-text pairs are asymmetric: an image contains many describable attributes, while its caption usually mentions only one aspect. Two images can be visually similar while their paired captions describe different aspects such as object category, background, color, or artistic style. Enforcing global distance consistency over such pairs can inject semantic noise into the representation space.","importance":"The main relevance is in objective design for vision-language pretraining. The paper does not propose a new backbone or a new data scaling recipe. Its useful point is more specific: regularization in multimodal contrastive learning should respect the one-to-many nature of image-caption supervision. This matters for CLIP-like dual encoders and also for broader multimodal pretraining, where alignment losses can be harmful if they ignore what part of one modality is actually supervised by the other.","technical_approach":"AspectCLIP replaces unconstrained global consistency with aspect-guided consistency. It first clusters captions using SimCSE embeddings, relying on text semantics to identify the aspect of the image being described. Samples within the same cluster are treated as aspect-coherent, while samples from different clusters are treated as potentially aspect-divergent. The regularizer is then split into two regimes. Within a cluster, the method applies full cyclic consistency at the sample level, strengthening fine-grained geometric alignment for a shared descriptive aspect. Across clusters, it avoids direct sample-to-sample constraints and instead aligns samples with cluster prototypes, which gives a weaker and smoother cross-aspect constraint. Compared with CyCLIP-style global regularization, the key change is not the presence of a consistency term, but the decision rule for when strict consistency is semantically appropriate.","experiments":"The experiments evaluate standard CLIP capabilities, including zero-shot classification, zero-shot retrieval, and linear probing, together with representation consistency metrics. The available text states that AspectCLIP consistently improves over traditional consistency regularizers and yields higher consistency scores, suggesting a more structured shared embedding space. The important ablations are expected to test text clustering, intra-cluster regularization, inter-cluster prototype alignment, and the difference between aspect-guided and global constraints. $\\textit{","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-14T18:39:29Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2607_13188.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有 被揭开就无法回退（remask），导致跨模态矛盾在采样过程中被锁定、无法自纠正。论文要解决的是“文图真正并行、同一去噪步内双向耦合、且能撤销错误承诺”的联合理解与生成问题。","importance":"对跨模态统一预训练（尤其是 unified vocabulary 下 AR+diffusion / masked LM 层面，训练目标和 loss 没有改动，对预训练范式本身的改动是中等量级。","technical_approach":"提出 Self-Correcting Coupled Markov Jump Processes（SC-CMJP），核心有两点：（1）跨模态耦合的转移率——在每个连续时间跳跃步内，一个模态从 mask 状态揭开成具体 token 的 rate，不是只取决于自身分支的置信度，而是由另一模态的 unmasking confidence 和 cross-modal attention 权重共同调制；文本模态的去噪速度会根据图像模态的置信度自适应放慢或加快，反之亦然。（2）允许 remask 的 birth-death 跳跃——借鉴 ReMDM 的做法，把标准 absorbing-state MDM 的单向 unmask 过程扩展为可揭开、可重新 mask 的双向过程，一旦跨模态证据表明先前的承诺有矛盾，就能回退已生成的 token，而不是让错误一路积累。在此框架上实现了 CO²Jump 采样器：不修改模型参数、不引入额外 evaluator、每步只需一次 forward pass，直接从 frozen MDM 的 cross-attention 中取出耦合信号，在连续时间 MJP 框架里做耦合的 (MDM) samplers for joint text-image generation either decode modalities sequentially (text first, image later) or run nominally parallel branches that share only the previous-step state, with no visibility into each other's latest decisions within the same denoising step. Combined with standard MDM's inability to remask once a token is unmasked, cross-modal contradictions are introduced and then locked in for the rest of the trajectory. The paper targets truly concurrent, within-step, bidirectionally coupled text-image understanding and generation with the ability to undo mistaken commitments.","importance":"Relevant to unified multimodal pretraining, especially the masked/discrete-diffusion branch of unified-vocabulary AR+diffusion models. The paper makes a sharp observation: a shared backbone and a parallel schedule do not, by themselves, produce coupled multimodal generation; within-step information flow and reversibility are essential. The contribution is primarily at the sampler / inference level rather than the training objective, so its impact on pretraining recipes is moderate, but it gives a concrete, training-free mechanism for coupling modalities that is worth considering when designing unified MDM-style multimodal decoders.","technical_approach":"The authors propose Self-Correcting Coupled Markov Jump Processes (SC-CMJP). Two core ideas: (1) coupled transition rates — within each continuous-time jump step, the rate at which a masked token in one modality is revealed to a concrete token is modulated not only by that modality's own confidence, but also by the other modality's unmasking confidence, weighted by cross-modal attention extracted from the same forward pass; the unmasking schedule of each modality adapts to the confidence of the other. (2) Remasking via a birth-death jump process — extending the standard absorbing-state (mask→token only) MDM into a bidirectional unmask-remask process in the spirit of ReMDM, so earlier commitments can be retracted when cross-modal evidence turns against them. On top of this they build CO²Jump, a training-free sampler that requires no architectural changes, no auxiliary verifier, and a single forward pass per denoising step; coupling signals are read directly from cross-attention of a frozen MDM.","experiments":"Evaluation on three tasks: an extended ImgEditBench protocol with grounded mAP-style metrics for image editing, and two newly constructed, algorithmically verifiable visual-reasoning tasks where text and image are logically interlocked — JMaze (maze solving) and JNono (nonogram solving) — supported by three curated corpora: JEdit-1M, JMaze-200K, JNono-200K. CO²Jump consistently improves joint text-image metrics over existing samplers (standard MDM sampler of Sahoo et al., ReMDM, and a parallel multimodal MDM baseline), with gains also on individual modalities. Performance scales monotonically with the number of denoising steps, suggesting coupling benefits compound along the trajectory. Ablations indicate that both cross-modal rate coupling and remasking are necessary for the gains.","reading_guide":"Skim. Focus on: the diagnosis in Section 3 of why 'parallel' MDM samplers are not truly coupled (lack of within-step information exchange plus no remask), the concrete equations showing how transition rates are coupled via cross-modal attention, and the extension from absorbing-only to birth-death jumps. In experiments, prioritize the maze/nonogram ablations because these logically interlocked tasks give the cleanest evidence that cross-modal correction is actually occurring; image-editing numbers are more metric- and prompt-sensitive.
