However, existing approaches use consensus only in restricted forms: as a filter that selects solutions for fine-tuning, as a preference between answers, or as a scalar reward for reinforcement learning, discarding most of the information th","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-15T09:39:32Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_13643.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体但重要的问题：在没有标注答案、没有外部 verifier、也不做长程 RL 的条件下，怎样把模型自己的“多数一致”信号，变成足够密、足够稳定的训练监督。已有无标签方法大多只把 consensus 当成筛子、偏好对，或一个标量奖励；信息利用很粗，而且 RL 方案通常需要反复采样与多轮优化。Canon 的切入点是把 consensus 提升为一种 privileged context：不是只告诉模型“哪个答案更可能对”，而是让一个冻结 teacher 在“已知共识解”的条件下，对学生当前 rollout 的每个 token 给出分布级监督，从而把无标签自提升从稀疏奖励改成 token-level distillation。","importance":"对预训练/后训练接口的意义主要有三层。第一，它说明无标签推理增强不一定要走 RL 这条高成本路径，dense distillation 也能吃到大部分收益，而且训练时间明显更短。第二，它把“self-consistency 只是 test-time aggregation 技巧”推进到“self-consistency 可以转成训练信号”，这对 unlabeled continual post-training、test-time adaptation、以及用模型自身生成信号做 mid-train/annealing 都有启发。第三，论文给出了一个很实用的适用性判断：当基础模型的 majority answer 往往正确但置信度不高时，Canon 最有效；当 majority 已经饱和，或多数答案稳定地错时，收益会消失甚至转负。这一点比单纯报平均分更有价值，因为它直接关系到什么时候该做自蒸馏，什么时候该停。","technical_approach":"方法本身不复杂，但设计很讲究。对每个无标签 prompt，先从当前策略采样 N 条解答轨迹，抽取最终答案，取多数答案作为 consensus answer，再从达到该答案的 rollout 中选一条 consensus solution。接着复制一个冻结快照作为 teacher，并把 prompt 与这条 consensus solution 一起喂给 teacher，使 teacher 处在一种“知道共识推理路径”的 privileged 状态。学生则只基于原始 prompt，对自己某条采样轨迹上的 token 做预测。训练目标不是 hard target CE，而是 teacher 与 student 在这些 token 位置上的全词表 Jensen–Shannon divergence。这里有几个关键点。其一，teacher 只在条件信息上更强，参数不更新，因此训练稳定，避免了 on-policy RL 的高方差。其二，监督落在学生自己采样出的 token 上，而不是强行模仿 teacher 的整条链路，减少了 exposure mismatch，也保留了学生原有解题风格。其三，使用 JSD 而不是单向 KL，目的是让分布匹配更平滑，避免 teacher 过度尖锐时把错误共识放大。其四，整个流程一轮生成即可同时提供 consensus 和蒸馏载体，训练只做 1 epoch，计算开销明显低于需要反复 rollout 的 test-time RL。论文还设置了 oracle 版本：teacher 条件化在 gold-verified solution 上，用来衡量“共识作为伪 privileged context”距离真实 gold context 还有多远。这个对照很关键，因为它把方法上限刻画得比较清楚。","experiments":"实验分成 transductive 和 inductive 两种。transductive 是直接在测试集 prompt 上做无标签训练，再在同一批 prompt 上评估；这是 test-time RL 常用设定。inductive 则在一组无标签 prompt 上训练，去看对不同、甚至更难 benchmark 的迁移。论文的主结论是：在作者比较的 label-free 方法里，Canon 的 accuracy-compute 比最好。文中给出的代表性结果是，transductive 场景下，在竞赛数学和研究生级科学问答上，pass@1 最多提升约 12 个点，而训练耗时大约 1 小时 GPU；对比的 RL 替代方法通常要 6–10 小时。与 oracle gold-conditioned teacher 相比，Canon 在 AMC 上只差约 0.5 个点，在 AIME 2024 上差约 3 个点，在 GPQA 上差约 8 个点，说明模型自身 consensus 已经能恢复 gold privileged context 的相当一部分价值。更重要的是，收益不是简单把概率质量重新压到模型原本就会的答案上。论文检查了“32 次采样里从未做对过的问题”，发现训练后模型能新解出一部分此前完全不会的问题；在未饱和 benchmark 上，多数投票本身也变得更准，说明底层策略确实被改善了。分析部分还给出 per-prompt 规律：当 base model 的 majority answer 正确但不够稳时，增益最大；当问题已接近饱和时，几乎无收益；当 majority 高置信但错误时，Canon 可能被错误共识带偏。这一条基本决定了方法的边界条件。摘要里没有完整列出参数量、N 的默认值、各 benchmark 全部数字和消融表，但从全文结构看，关键消融应包括 teacher 条件化方式、共识解选择、训练 epoch、与 SFT/RL 基线的 compute 对比，以及 transductive/inductive 两种设定下的迁移表现。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三件事。第一，看方法部分里 teacher/student 的条件差异和损失定义，重点理解为什么它是 privileged-context distillation，而不是普通 self-training。第二，看 transductive 与 inductive 两套实验，因为很多 test-time training 方法只在 transductive 设定里好看，Canon 的价值在于作者专门验证了迁移。第三，看适用性分析，尤其是按 majority correctness / confidence 分桶后的结果，这部分最能帮助判断方法能否迁到自己的任务。若时间有限，建议优先读引言、方法总图、主结果表、oracle 对照、以及 Section 5.2 关于 consensus 何时可靠的讨论，预计 25–35 分钟能抓住主线。若要深挖，值得继续看 JSD 设计、学生在自身 token 上蒸馏的理由，以及“新解出此前 32 次都没解出的题”这一分析是如何定义和统计的。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise question: how can unlabeled agreement among a model’s own sampled solutions be converted into a dense and trainable supervision signal, without gold answers, external verifiers, or expensive RL loops? Prior label-free methods mostly use consensus in a lossy way: as a filter for SFT, as pairwise preference data, or as a scalar pseudo-reward. Canon treats consensus as privileged context instead. Rather than only saying which final answer is more plausible, it conditions a frozen teacher on a consensus-reaching solution and distills token-level distributions into the student over the student’s own rollouts.","importance":"The main value is at the pretrain–posttrain interface. First, it shows that unlabeled reasoning improvement does not have to be mediated by RL; dense self-distillation can recover much of the gain at much lower optimization cost. Second, it upgrades self-consistency from an inference-time aggregation trick into a reusable training signal, which matters for unlabeled continual adaptation, test-time training, and possibly mid-training phases where labels are scarce but model-generated confidence structure is available. Third, the paper gives a useful operating condition: Canon helps most when the base model’s majority answer is often correct but not yet confident; it helps little on saturated prompts and can hurt when consensus is confidently wrong. That boundary condition is more informative than average benchmark gains.","technical_approach":"$16","experiments":"$17","reading_guide":"For a quick read, focus on three pieces. First, the method section defining the teacher–student asymmetry and the JSD loss; that is where the paper departs from ordinary self-training. Second, the transductive versus inductive experiments; many test-time adaptation methods look good only in the former, so transfer is the real stress test here. Third, read the applicability analysis that buckets prompts by consensus correctness and confidence, because that tells you when the method should or should not be deployed. If time is limited, read the introduction, the method figure, the main result tables, the oracle comparison, and the section discussing when consensus is reliable. That should take about 30 minutes. If reading more deeply, inspect why distillation is done on student tokens, why JSD was chosen, and how the paper defines and measures newly solved problems that were unsolved in 32 pre-training attempts.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读方法图和训练目标即可；它把 self-consistency 变成 dense teacher，值得放进内部 RLVR 消融。","en":"Read the method diagram and objective; turning self-consistency into a dense teacher is worth testing in internal RLVR ablations."},"metadata":{"institutions":["ILLC, University of Amsterdam","ILCC, University of Edinburgh"],"authors":["John Gkountouras","Josip Jukić","Ivan Titov"],"notable_authors":["Ivan Titov"],"tags":["self-distillation","reasoning","self-consistency","post-training","dense-supervision"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13643","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.13643","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.13501","title":"LAPO: Leave-One-Turn Attribution for Self-Generated Process Rewards in Multi-Turn Search Reasoning","abstract":"Reinforcement learning for multi-turn search reasoning typically relies on terminal outcome rewards, which cannot distinguish useful, redundant, and harmful intermediate interactions. We propose LAPO, a self-generated process-supervision method based on backward leave-one-turn attribution. For each search turn, LAPO replaces the turn and its retrieval observation with a fixed [DELETE] placeholder and measures the resulting change in the current policy's mean log-likelihood of the gold answer. Th","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-15T06:55:28Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_13501.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"该工作解决多轮搜索推理 RL 中的轨迹内 credit assignment 问题。只用最终答案对错作为 reward，会把同一条轨迹中的所有搜索动作一起奖励或惩罚，无法区分有用检索、冗余检索和误导检索。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接关系不在基座预训练本身，而在 post-train 信号构造。它提供了一个值得借鉴的思路：过程监督不一定要来自外部 reward model、teacher 或 verifier，也可以从当前 policy 对 gold answer 的条件似然变化中构造。这个设计对 RL 训练中的信号质量、过程奖励自举、以及搜索增强推理的训练稳定性有启发。贡献量级是一个具体 RL recipe，适合泛读，不属于通用预训练方法。","technical_approach":"LOTAPO 使用 backward leave-one-turn attribution 构造自生成过程奖励。给定完整多轮搜索轨迹，每个有效搜索 turn 包含模型生成的 search action 和环境返回的 observation。方法依次把某个 search turn 及其 observation 替换成固定的 [DELETE] 占位符，然后在保留下游交互不变的条件下，计算当前 policy 对 gold answer 的平均 log-likelihood 变化。若删除某个 turn 后 gold answer 似然下降，说明该 turn 对最终回答有正贡献；若似然上升，则说明该 turn 可能冗余或有害。这个 Answer-Likelihood Gain 被用作 turn-level attribution。为降低噪声，方法还做 robust scaling、tanh 有界变换、group normalization，并加入 sign-consistency gating：只有归一化后的 advantage 与原始 likelihood gain 在方向上一致时，才保留该过程信号。最终，搜索 turn 的 policy tokens 同时接收 GRPO 的终局 advantage 和 LOTAPO 的过程 advantage；最终答案 turn 仍只使用终局 reward。","experiments":"实验覆盖七个知识密集型问答数据集，评估多轮搜索推理场景下的 RL 训练效果。摘要和正文片段表明，LOTAPO 相比只用 outcome reward 的训练能提供更细粒度的中间步骤反馈，并且不需要额外训练过程奖励模型、教师模型、verifier 或 LLM judge。关键实验应关注三类结果：最终 QA accuracy 或 exact match 的提升；去掉 sign-consistency gating、只用原始 likelihood gain、去掉 group normalization 等消融；以及不同 turn 位置的 attribution 是否能识别早期关键证据。当前给出的全文内容未包含具体数值表，因此不补写具体提升幅度或模型规模。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25–35 分钟。优先看 Method 中 leave-one-turn 替换方式、Answer-Likelihood Gain 的定义、归一化与 sign-consistency gating 的公式。实验部分重点看 outcome-only GRPO 与 LOTAPO 的对比，以及 gating 和归一化的消融。若关注 RLHF/RLVR 或搜索增强推理，值得细看它如何避免外部过程奖励模型带来的分布偏移；若只关注基座预训练，可以读到方法公式和主要消融即可。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets credit assignment in reinforcement learning for multi-turn search reasoning. Terminal answer correctness gives the same trajectory-level signal to all intermediate search actions, so useful, redundant, and harmful search turns are reinforced or penalized together.","importance":"Its relevance is mainly to post-training and RL signal construction rather than base-model pretraining. Outcome rewards provide reliable supervision for short-horizon reasoning, but become sparse and high-variance as trajectories grow to tens or hundreds of tool calls. They can also be misleading: a failed rollout may contain many useful actions that move the agent closer to the goal, yet outcome-only training ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-15T16:16:42Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_13988.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"长 horizon 智能体的强化学习训练中，仅使用最终结果奖励存在稀疏性高、方差大的问题：会给同一条轨迹上的所有中间工具调用分配相同的信用，无法区分对解题有贡献的动作、冗余动作和导致失败的关键错误。现有过程监督方案要么需要昂贵的步骤级人工标注，要么依赖强能力的LLM裁判，或是需要单独训练的过程奖励模型，后者还容易出现和最终结果正确性偏离的漂移问题，缺少不依赖额外标注和额外模型的轻量密集信用分配方案。","importance":"为可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）扩展到长序列智能体场景提供了低 overhead 的信用分配思路，证明不需要SFT冷启动、专项中训、过程奖励模型或强裁判，仅靠和最终结果严格对齐的密集步级奖励，就能通过纯RL让基础模型学会复杂的长程工具使用能力，对长序列能力的RL激发、智能体训练信号构造都有实际参考价值。","technical_approach":"核心设计是用冻结的参考模型作为稳定的解题进度探针，而非裁判。首先将智能体交互轨迹按工具调用边界切分为状态转移序列，对每个轨迹前缀，计算冻结参考模型输出黄金答案的对数概率，将相邻前缀的对数概率差（时序差分TD值）作为当前工具调用步的回合级奖励：如果工具返回的观测提升了答案的可预测性就给正奖励，无信息增益就给接近零的奖励，导致轨迹偏离目标就给负奖励。这种TD奖励天然满足望远镜求和性质，累计值和最终结果严格对齐，不会给冗余步骤分配虚高信用。最终训练奖励将该密集TD信号和标准结果级优势结合，既保留可验证最终结果的锚定作用，又为每一步动作分配合理信用，全程不需要额外标注、裁判模型或过程奖励模型。","experiments":"在长程深度搜索任务上开展测试，训练全程采用纯RL设置：无SFT冷启动、无智能体专项中训、无实时网页训练数据、无LLM强裁判、无过程奖励模型。闭域网页场景BrowseComp-Plus上，Qwen3-4B得分从7.2提升到35.6，Qwen3-30B-A3B得分从8.4提升到42.6；训练得到的30B-A3B智能体可零样本迁移到开域网页场景，在BrowseComp得分12.9、GAIA得分52.0、xbench-DeepSearch得分45.0。训练曲线显示，相比纯结果奖励训练，TRACE方法性能启动更早、收敛速度更快。","reading_guide":"泛读优先看第1节问题定义、第3节方法框架，重点理解冻结参考模型作为对数概率探针的设计逻辑、TD回合奖励的望远镜对齐性质，扫过第4节的核心结果表和训练曲线对比即可，熟悉KL正则化智能体RL的读者可跳过第2节预备知识部分，预计阅读时间15-20分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Outcome-only reinforcement learning for long-horizon LLM agents suffers from sparse, high-variance training signals: it assigns identical credit to all intermediate tool calls in a trajectory, failing to distinguish productive, evidence-gathering actions from redundant operations and critical missteps that cause failure. Existing process supervision pipelines rely on expensive step-level annotations, capable LLM judges, or separately trained process reward models, which often drift from alignment with ground-truth outcome correctness. No existing lightweight dense credit assignment method works without these extra supervision components.","importance":"TRACE offers a low-overhead credit assignment recipe to extend reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) to long-horizon agent settings. It demonstrates that pure RL, without SFT cold start, specialized agent mid-training, process reward models, or strong judges, can endow base models with complex long-horizon tool use capabilities when paired with dense, outcome-aligned step rewards. The design provides practical reference for RL-based elicitation of long-sequence capabilities and agent training signal construction.","technical_approach":"The core design uses a frozen reference model as a stable progress probe, rather than an explicit judge. Rollouts are split into state transitions at tool call boundaries, and each trajectory prefix is scored by the frozen reference model's log probability of the gold answer. Turn-level rewards are defined as the temporal difference (TD) of this score between adjacent prefixes: a tool call receives positive reward if its returned observation increases answer predictability, near-zero reward if it adds no task-relevant information, and negative reward if it shifts the trajectory away from the goal. The TD rewards follow a telescoping sum property that strictly aligns cumulative credit with the final outcome, eliminating spurious positive credit for redundant actions. The final training objective combines this dense TD signal with standard outcome-level advantage, keeping verifiable task success as the anchor while providing fine-grained step supervision.","experiments":"Evaluated on long-horizon deep search tasks across closed-web and open-web settings, with a pure RL training setup: no SFT cold start, no agent-specific mid-training, no live-web training data, no LLM judges, no separate process reward models. On closed-web BrowseComp-Plus, TRACE improves Qwen3-4B from 7.2 to 35.6 and Qwen3-30B-A3B from 8.4 to 42.6. The trained 30B-A3B agent transfers zero-shot to open-web settings, reaching 12.9 on BrowseComp, 52.0 on GAIA, and 45.0 on xbench-DeepSearch. Training curves show earlier performance takeoff and faster convergence compared to outcome-only RL baselines.","reading_guide":"For skimming, focus on the problem framing in Section 1 and method design in Section 3, paying particular attention to the logic of using a frozen reference model as a log-probability probe and the telescoping alignment property of TD turn rewards. Readers familiar with KL-regularized agent RL can skip the preliminaries in Section 2. Review core result tables and training curve comparisons in Section 4 for empirical validation. This is relevant to RLVR, reasoning-model monitoring, and test-time compute allocation.","technical_approach":"The authors introduce a three-stage calibration framework: pre-CoT confidence for difficulty estimation, intra-CoT confidence for early termination, and post-CoT trace confidence for answer aggregation. Constrained decoding intervenes earlier by rejecting invalid tokens during sampling, but requires white-box model access and costly reimplementation for semantic constraints.","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-15T15:00:22Z","category_id":"coding_agent","cover_url":"/covers/2607_13921.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.PL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有LLM代码生成的两种编译器反馈范式各有本质缺陷：生成后反馈需等待完整代码输出才返回错误，首个不可恢复错误之后生成的所有token全部浪费，且批量累积的错误信息过多会影响模型理解；约束解码需要白盒访问模型的token分布、重实现目标语言的语义规则，无法用于仅提供API的黑盒模型，且不会给模型提供解释性的错误反馈。","importance":"该工作提出了介于生成后反馈和约束解码之间的新反馈范式，为代码LLM的训练数据合成、推理时纠错回路设计提供了新思路。其黑盒兼容的细粒度编译反馈能力，可直接用于构造更高质量的代码微调数据，或优化代码Agent的推理时自我修正机制。","technical_approach":"提出生成式编译框架，核心是sealor（密封器）概念：一种将部分程序补全为完整程序的变换，通过填充缺失的语法结构、插入类型合法的占位符，让现有编译器可以直接检查未完成的部分程序。合格的sealor需满足两个性质：完备性（存在合法补全的部分程序不会被误判为错误）和可靠性（被判定错误的部分程序确实无法补全为合法程序）。先在Featherweight Rust（Rust所有权与借用机制的精简演算）上实现sealor并形式化证明其性质，用Lean定理证明器机械化验证；再扩展到真实Rust实现，保留已生成的语法结构，插入最少占位符，将类型、借用、生命周期等语义检查全部委托给官方rustc编译器。","experiments":"在7个前沿黑盒和开源LLM上评估，覆盖两个Rust仓库级代码生成任务：C到Rust的翻译、基于最新更新的库API生成代码。结果显示，与生成后反馈相比，生成式编译的实时反馈回路在大多数模型-任务配置下进一步减少了编译器错误数量，提升了功能正确率。错误分析表明，生成式编译提供的诊断信息更聚焦于错误源，且在文件完全生成前很早就可以获得。","reading_guide":"泛读重点看第3节的sealor定义和生成式编译核心框架，第6节真实Rust的sealor工程实现，以及第7节的实验结果对比。第4、5节的形式化证明部分如果不关注程序语言理论可跳过。预计阅读时间1-1.5小时。","authors_note":"ETH Zurich、INSAIT、加州大学伯克利分校；作者：Niels Mündler-Sasahara、Hristo Venev、Dawn Song、Martin Vechev、Jingxuan He"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Two dominant compiler feedback paradigms for LLM code generation have critical limitations: post-generation feedback waits for a complete file before returning errors, wasting all tokens generated after the first unrecoverable error and overwhelming the model with batched diagnostics; constrained decoding requires white-box access to the model's token distribution and reimplementation of target language semantics, making it incompatible with black-box API models and unable to provide explanatory error feedback to the model.","importance":"This work introduces a middle-ground feedback paradigm between post-generation feedback and constrained decoding, offering new directions for training data synthesis and inference-time error correction loop design for code LLMs. Its black-box-compatible fine-grained compiler feedback can be directly used to construct higher-quality code fine-tuning data or optimize inference-time self-correction mechanisms for code agents.","technical_approach":"The proposed generative compilation framework centers on the sealor: a transformation that closes a partial program into a complete one by filling missing syntactic structure and inserting well-typed placeholders, allowing off-the-shelf compilers to check incomplete partial programs directly. A valid sealor must satisfy two properties: completeness (partial programs with valid completions are never falsely rejected) and soundness (rejected partial programs truly have no valid extension). The framework is first instantiated on Featherweight Rust, a compact calculus for Rust's ownership and borrowing discipline, with its properties formally proved and mechanized in the Lean theorem prover. It is then extended to real Rust, preserving generated syntax, inserting minimal placeholders, and delegating all type, borrow, and lifetime checking to the official rustc compiler.","experiments":"Evaluated across 7 frontier black-box and open-weight LLMs on two repository-level Rust code generation tasks: C-to-Rust translation and code generation against recently updated library APIs. new memory store. It targets a more basic layer: how an LLM agent should decide when to retrieve memory, what kind of memory to retrieve, how much to retrieve, whether to re-retrieve after getting stuck, and when to consolidate or forget old memory. Most prior systems hard-code these choices with fixed heuristics such as one retrieval per step, one query template, one top-k, or one graph-hop depth. The alternative line, exemplified by MemGPT-style designs, lets an LLM act as the controller, but that adds an extra model call for each memory operation. The core problem here is whether memory access can be turned into a lightweight, online-learned, backend-agnostic control process without adding any extra LLM calls.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense. Second, some gains often attributed to stronger models or richer memory stores may actually come from better timing and better choice of memory operations. That matters for retrieval-augmented training, test-time adaptation, long-context modeling, and learned episodic memory. The contribution is best understood as a clean systems abstraction: a reusable control layer that can sit on top of many memory backends, rather than a new modeling paradigm.","technical_approach":"$1d","experiments":"$1e","reading_guide":"For a skim, focus on three parts.
